[2026-10-12 17:27:45] RESULT: 470	- The place list is not exhaustive: 11.1% of posts name no oblast, and ambiguous place names are left out
471	  on purpose.
472	- Sep 4–5 is 48 origins per oblast.
473	
474	## Limitations and what we would do next
475	Limitations:
476	- **Volunteer vs official agreement** fell from κ 0.985 (2022Q3) to 0.448 (2026Q3). About 0.025
477	  of that is the Lviv/Volyn parser bug; the rest is unexplained.
478	- **Western oblasts:** Lviv, Ivano-Frankivsk and Zakarpattia had one new alert each in the 24 test
479	  days. Their within-oblast AUCs are close to noise. In the five furthest west, the model
480	  predicts about 0.06–0.07 at H=6 where 0.01–0.03 happened.
481	- **Sep 4–5:** two days with 7-day features scaled from the observed hours. Every predictor scores
482	  lower there, and two days can't say why.
483	- **Thresholds** come from a validation fit on train minus the last week, not from the model
484	  scored on test. The frequency baseline's threshold has the same approximation.
485	- **The official feed stopped on 2026-09-07,** so the test window ends a month before the pull. It
486	  can't move until upstream recovers.
487	- **The raion switch (Jul–Dec 2025)** changed what an alert means. The half-of-raions rule keeps
488	  base rates roughly continuous for most oblasts but not all (Kherson), and training mixes both
489	  regimes. Training only on 2026 neither clearly helped nor hurt.
490	
491	Next:
492	- Weekly refits through the test window, to see whether level drift

[2026-10-12 17:27:58] RESULT: """What the model would have said at a past hour, from the data known at that hour only.

    python scripts/predict.py --at "2026-09-05 14:00" --oblast Kyivska
    python scripts/predict.py --at "2026-09-05 17:00+03:00" --all
    python scripts/predict.py --now --oblast Kharkivska

For origin t (a whole hour, UTC unless the time carries an offset) prints the probability that a new
alert starts in (t, t+H] for H = 1, 3, 6 (target (b)), from the LightGBM models scripts/evaluate.py
saved in models/, next to the three baselines and to what actually happened.

--now checks the official alert channel (https://t.me/s/air_alert_ua, public page, no token) for anything newer than our
snapshot. If there is nothing newer, or the channel cannot be reached, it says so in one sentence and shows the forecast for
the latest hour we have, labelled as such. It does not turn live posts into forecasts, and there is no other fallback.

The inputs are recomputed from the alert rows as they looked at t: only alerts that had started by
t, with the end of any alert still running at t unknown (the same cut scripts/check_no_peeking.py
checks against the full-data features). Times before the end of training (models/info.json) were
part of the training rows: the output says so, because the model has seen what happened next.
"""

import pandas as pd

from .load import OFFICIAL_OUTAGES, PERMANENT_ALERT_OBLASTS, data_end, load_official
from .panel import official_episodes, raion_list
from .targets import active_now, make_rows, touches, y_active, y_start

HORIZONS = [1, 3, 6]
TEST_DAYS = 30       # test = last 30 full days of official data
THRESHOLD_DAYS = 7   # thresholds (and early stopping) are chosen on the last week of train
# rows must have [t-24h, t+H] clear of outages: same-hour-yesterday and the 24h features need it.
# 7-day features are computed over the observed part of their window instead (src/features.py).
EVAL_LOOKBACK = pd.Timedelta(hours=24)
# features look back up to 7 days, so no row is used before 7 days of history exist
FEATURE_HISTORY = pd.Timedelta(days=7)


def load_inputs():
    """Cleaned official rows without permanent-alert oblasts, and the fixed raion list."""
    official = load_official()
    official = official[~official["oblast"].isin(PERMANENT_ALERT_OBLASTS)]
    return official, raion_list(official)


def build_rows(official=None, raions=None):
    """Complete hourly grid of (oblast, t) with "now" and both targets for every H.

    "now" comes from raw episodes (before joining restarts): at t nobody knows yet whether a
    restart will follow. Targets come from the joined episodes.
    """
    if official is None:
        official, raions = load_inputs()
    episodes, raw = offic
